  <div class="clearfix container-xl tmp-px-5 tmp-mt-4 page" data-hpc="true" id="wiki-wrapper">
    <div class="d-flex flex-column flex-md-row gh-header">
      <h1 class="flex-auto min-width-0 mb-2 mb-md-0 mr-0 mr-md-2 gh-header-title">Release Planning</h1>

        <div class="mt-0 mt-lg-1 flex-shrink-0 gh-header-actions">
          <a href="#wiki-pages-box" class="d-md-none ">Jump to bottom</a>

        </div>
    </div>

      <div class="mt-2 mt-md-1 tmp-pb-3 gh-header-meta">
        Guillaume Smet edited this page <relative-time datetime="2026-11-04T14:55:50Z" class="no-wrap">Nov 4, 2026</relative-time>
        &middot;
        <a href="/quarkusio/quarkus/wiki/Release-Planning/_history" data-view-component="true" class="Link Link--underline">127 revisions</a>
      </div>

    <div id="wiki-content" class="tmp-mt-4">
      <div data-view-component="true" class="Layout Layout--flowRow-until-md Layout--sidebarPosition-end Layout--sidebarPosition-flowRow-end">
  <div data-view-component="true" class="Layout-main">          <div id="wiki-body" class="gollum-markdown-content">
              <div class="markdown-body">
                <p>The <a href="https://github.com/quarkusio/quarkus/blob/master/COMMITTERS.adoc#release-schedule">COMMITTERS guide</a> contains useful information about the release schedule.</p>
<p>Discussions about the release planning in relation to the Quarkus Platform are taking place here: <a href="https://groups.google.com/g/quarkus-platform-coordination" rel="nofollow">https://groups.google.com/g/quarkus-platform-coordination</a>.</p>
<p>Releases get announced here: <a href="https://groups.google.com/g/quarkus-dev" rel="nofollow">https://groups.google.com/g/quarkus-dev</a>.</p>
<p><strong>Everything that needs to be in a release needs to be merged until the day before the release date.</strong></p>
<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-note"><p class="markdown-alert-title"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-info mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M0 8a8 8 0 1 1 16 0A8 8 0 0 1 0 8Zm8-6.5a6.5 6.5 0 1 0 0 13 6.5 6.5 0 0 0 0-13ZM6.5 7.75A.75.75 0 0 1 7.25 7h1a.75.75 0 0 1 .75.75v2.75h.25a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-2a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.5h.25v-2h-.25a.75.75 0 0 1-.75-.75ZM8 6a1 1 0 1 1 0-2 1 1 0 0 1 0 2Z"></path></svg>Note</p><p><code>.0</code> releases are made in two phases:</p>
<ul>
<li>The Core release is released first. It contains all the artifacts from the <a href="https://github.com/quarkusio/quarkus">Core repository</a>. Extension maintainers usually depend on this release.</li>
<li>The <a href="https://github.com/quarkusio/quarkus-platform">Quarkus Platform</a> is released one week after so that Platform members have the time to contribute updates to the Platform. The Platform release is what is consumed by end users (via <code>io.quarkus.platform:quarkus-bom</code>) and we announce the new Quarkus release only then.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">4.3 LTS (Proposal)</h2><a id="user-content-43-lts-proposal" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 4.3 LTS (Proposal)" href="#43-lts-proposal"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul>
<li>Core CR1 on March 17th 2027</li>
<li>Core Final on March 24th 2027</li>
